Next step decision
P4.10.0-C8-I-D4-L-L-O-BP - Controlled Activity Intake First Real Auth Session Runtime Read Contract Verification

Contract layer name
controlled-activity-intake-first-real-auth-session-runtime-read-contract-v1

Reason for O-BO
O-BN approved only a contract/design step for the first real Auth0/session runtime read.
O-BO defines the contract for that first runtime-read diagnostic boundary.
O-BO does not implement the diagnostic endpoint and does not execute a real Auth0/session runtime read.

Current verified state
- serverSideAuthSession.ts remains static/no-I-O.
- serverSideAuthSessionRuntime.ts remains static/no-I-O.
- serverSideAuthSessionRuntimeConnection.ts remains static/no-I-O.
- serverSideAuthSessionRuntimeBridge.ts remains static/no-I-O.
- controlled-intake route.ts does not call the bridge.
- controlled-intake route.ts remains no-write preview only.
- productionWriteEnabled=false remains in controlled-intake route.ts.
- no Supabase app-user mapping read is approved.
- no production activity_events insert is approved.

Future diagnostic endpoint path
The first real session-read implementation may create only:
- src/app/api/activity/debug/real-auth-session-runtime-read-test/route.ts

The first real session-read implementation must not modify:
- src/app/api/activity/controlled-intake/route.ts
- lib/activity/controlledIntake/serverSideAuthSession.ts
- lib/activity/controlledIntake/serverSideAuthSessionRuntime.ts
- lib/activity/controlledIntake/serverSideAuthSessionRuntimeConnection.ts
- lib/activity/controlledIntake/serverSideAuthSessionRuntimeBridge.ts
- lib/activity/controlledIntake/serverSideAppUserMapping.ts
- lib/activity/controlledIntake/authContext.ts
- lib/activity/controlledIntake/productionAuthMapping.ts
- lib/activity/controlledIntake/persist.ts
- database schema
- migrations

Required diagnostic header
The future endpoint must require:
- x-controlled-intake-first-real-session-read-test: true

If this header is missing or not exactly true, the endpoint must return:
- HTTP 403
- ok=false
- code=CONTROLLED_INTAKE_FIRST_REAL_SESSION_DEBUG_HEADER_REQUIRED
- no Auth0/session helper call
- no DB/SQL/AI

Required explicit runtime-read approval
The future endpoint must require an explicit internal approval flag inside the implementation, not from the request body.
If the implementation flag is missing/false, the endpoint must return:
- HTTP 403
- ok=false
- code=CONTROLLED_INTAKE_FIRST_REAL_SESSION_READ_NOT_APPROVED
- no DB/SQL/AI

Required future Auth0/session boundary
The future endpoint may call only the approved project Auth0/session server helper.
The helper must be called only after the diagnostic header and explicit implementation approval pass.
The helper must be called only from the debug endpoint.
The helper output must be immediately sanitized before response or bridge use.

Required bridge path
The future endpoint should pass only sanitized session data into:
- buildControlledActivityIntakeServerSideAuthSessionRuntimeBridge

The bridge should then pass sanitized data through:
- serverSideAuthSessionRuntimeConnection.ts
- serverSideAuthSessionRuntime.ts
- serverSideAuthSession.ts

Required output success JSON
A successful future diagnostic response may return only:
- ok: true
- stage: "first_real_auth_session_runtime_read_test"
- layer: "controlled-activity-intake-first-real-auth-session-runtime-read-test-v1"
- requestSource
- sessionPresent: true
- provider: "auth0"
- authSubject
- email if available and safe
- identity from bridge:
  - provider
  - authSubject
  - email if available and safe
  - sessionId if available and safe
  - requestSource
- guardrails
- connectionBridgeStatus:
  - connectionOk
  - runtimeOk
  - staticIdentityOk

Required output failure JSON
A failure future diagnostic response may return only:
- ok: false
- stage: "first_real_auth_session_runtime_read_test"
- layer: "controlled-activity-intake-first-real-auth-session-runtime-read-test-v1"
- code
- message sanitized
- requestSource
- sessionPresent boolean if known
- provider if known and safe
- guardrails

Forbidden response fields
The future endpoint must never return:
- access_token
- refresh_token
- id_token
- token
- raw cookie
- raw session
- raw profile
- full Auth0 user object
- full provider payload
- roles
- permissions
- app metadata
- user metadata
- appUserId
- user_id
- actorId
- organizationId
- spaceId
- membership rows
- Supabase rows
- activity_events rows
- semantic candidates
- value objects
- state facts/deltas/snapshots

Required HTTP statuses
- 200 for successful sanitized identity read
- 401 for missing session
- 403 for missing diagnostic header or missing internal approval
- 500 only for sanitized unexpected helper/runtime failures
- 500 response must not expose raw exception payloads

Required failure codes
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_FIRST_REAL_SESSION_READ_NOT_APPROVED
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_FIRST_REAL_SESSION_DEBUG_HEADER_REQUIRED
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_FIRST_REAL_SESSION_BOUNDARY_UNAVAILABLE
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_FIRST_REAL_SESSION_READ_FAILED
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_FIRST_REAL_SESSION_UNSAFE_PAYLOAD
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_SERVER_SESSION_REQUIRED
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_AUTH_SUBJECT_REQUIRED
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_AUTH_SESSION_EXPIRED
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_AUTH_SESSION_INVALID
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_AUTH_PROVIDER_UNSUPPORTED
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_AUTH_BRIDGE_RUNTIME_FAILED
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_AUTH_BRIDGE_IDENTITY_FAILED
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_AUTH_BRIDGE_UNSAFE_RESULT

Required fail-closed behavior
- missing diagnostic header -> fail before session helper call
- diagnostic header not exactly true -> fail before session helper call
- missing internal runtime-read approval -> fail before session helper call
- missing project Auth0/session helper -> fail
- helper throws -> fail
- missing session -> fail
- expired session -> fail
- missing sub/authSubject -> fail
- blank sub/authSubject -> fail
- email-only identity -> fail
- unsupported provider -> fail
- unsafe token/raw-session payload -> fail
- bridge failure -> fail
- any uncertainty about identity anchor -> fail

Required guardrails in future endpoint response
- debugEndpointOnly=true
- diagnosticReadOnly=true
- realAuth0SessionReadAllowed=true only in the implementation/runtime test step
- realAuth0SessionReadExecuted=true only if the helper call actually executes
- auth0RuntimeReadExecuted=true only if the helper call actually executes
- controlledIntakeRouteChanged=false
- serverSideSessionReadOnly=true
- serverSideContextRequired=true
- clientOwnershipAccepted=false
- previewHeaderAcceptedForProductionWrite=false
- dbReadExecuted=false
- dbWriteExecuted=false
- sqlExecuted=false
- aiCallExecuted=false
- activityEventsInserted=false
- semanticCandidatesPersisted=false
- valueObjectsCreated=false
- stateFactsCreated=false
- stateDeltasCreated=false
- stateSnapshotsCreated=false

Required no-write/no-DB/no-AI implementation checks
The future implementation and verification must prove:
- controlled-intake route.ts unchanged
- productionWriteEnabled=false preserved in controlled-intake route.ts
- no Supabase client import
- no createClient
- no .from(
- no .insert(
- no .update(
- no .delete(
- no .rpc(
- no SQL text
- no OpenAI import
- no chat.completions
- no AI route call
- no category/value/state persistence
- no activity_events insert
- no app_users/users/actors/orgs/spaces/memberships read

Required first real read runtime verification
The future runtime verification must be browser-authenticated.
The browser must call the diagnostic endpoint with:
- x-controlled-intake-first-real-session-read-test: true

The verification must check:
- missing header returns 403 and no helper call
- valid header with authenticated browser session returns sanitized identity or controlled missing-session failure
- no tokens returned
- no raw session/profile returned
- no DB read/write
- no SQL
- no AI
- controlled-intake route.ts remains unchanged

Important separation
The first real session read is not app-user mapping.
The first real session read is not actor/org/space resolution.
The first real session read is not organization membership resolution.
The first real session read is not production trust for writes.
The first real session read only proves that a browser-authenticated request can produce a safe Auth0 subject through a no-write diagnostic boundary.

Recommended implementation sequence after O-BO
- O-BP: verify this contract.
- O-BQ: implementation design gate for the diagnostic endpoint.
- O-BR: static implementation of the diagnostic endpoint.
- O-BS: static verification of the diagnostic endpoint without executing Auth0/session read.
- O-BT: browser-authenticated first real Auth0/session runtime read verification, still no DB/SQL/AI/write.
- Route integration remains later and separate.

Local evidence collection note
O-BO collected local Auth0/session evidence with git grep only.
This is not an Auth0 runtime call.
This is not a session read.
This is not a Supabase read.
This is not SQL execution.
This is not a DB write.

Evidence count
- Local first real session-read contract evidence count: 2002

Important limitation after O-BO
O-BO creates only the contract.
It does not implement the diagnostic endpoint.
It does not execute a real Auth0/session runtime read.
It does not integrate anything into controlled-intake route.ts.
It does not read Supabase.
It does not approve production write mode.
It does not approve activity_events insert.
It does not approve AI or semantic persistence.

Still forbidden after O-BO
- Do not integrate runtime adapter into controlled-intake route.ts.
